};\n376\t});\n377\t"}]},"uuid":"29ab12e2-d69c-4e33-93ef-44eee3c34e43","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:43:11.351Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts","content":"// WEBSOCKET STREAMING ROUTE\n// GET /ws/streaming-deepgram - Deepgram Live proxy\n\nimport type { Context, Next } from 'hono';\nimport { upgradeWebSocket } from 'hono/bun';\nimport { generateRequestId, getClientIP } from '../lib/request-id';\nimport { computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost, creditsForCost } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport { validateAuth, type AuthContext } from '../middleware/auth';\nimport { deductCredits, validateCredits } from '../middleware/credits';\nimport { isIPBlocked } from '../lib/redis';\n\ninterface DeepgramLiveResponse {\n  type: string;\n  duration?: number;\n  is_final?: boolean;\n  speech_final?: boolean;\n  channel?: {\n    alternatives?: Array<{ transcript?: string }>;\n  };\n}\n\ninterface ReadyMessage {\n  type: 'ready';\n  sessionId: string;\n}\n\ninterface TranscriptMessage {\n  type: 'transcript';\n  text: string;\n  is_final: boolean;\n  speech_final: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface SessionCompleteMessage {\n  type: 'session_complete';\n  duration_seconds: number;\n  credits_used: number;\n}\n\ninterface ErrorMessage {\n  type: 'error';\n  message: string;\n}\n\ntype ServerMessage = ReadyMessage | TranscriptMessage | SessionCompleteMessage | ErrorMessage;\n\nconst STREAMING_SAMPLE_RATE = 16000;\nconst STREAMING_CHANNELS = 1;\nconst LINEAR16_BYTES_PER_SAMPLE = 2;\n\n// Minimum balance required to open a streaming session (~30s of Deepgram Nova-3 audio).\nconst STREAMING_MIN_BALANCE_SECONDS = 30;\n\nexport function minimumStreamingCredits(): number {\n  return creditsForCost(computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(STREAMING_MIN_BALANCE_SECONDS));\n}\n\n// Inbound audio limits — guard the Deepgram proxy against a misbehaving or\n// malicious client that pushes binary far faster (or larger) than the natural\n// 32 KB/s rate of 16 kHz mono linear16. Without these caps the client can grow\n// the outbound socket's buffer unbounded until the Fly machine OOMs.\n//\n// A single audio frame above 1 MB (~32 s of audio) is abnormal for streaming.\nconst MAX_AUDIO_MESSAGE_BYTES = 1 * 1024 * 1024;\n// Cumulative per-session cap (~52 min of 16 kHz mono linear16), well above any\n// real dictation session. Exceeding it closes the socket with 1009.\nconst MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES = 100 * 1024 * 1024;\n// If the upstream socket has more than this still buffered, the client is\n// outrunning Deepgram — drop the chunk instead of queueing more memory.\nconst MAX_DEEPGRAM_BUFFERED_BYTES = 1 * 1024 * 1024;\n\ninterface WSContext {\n  readyState: number;\n  send(data: string | ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array): void;\n  close(code?: number, reason?: string): void;\n}\n\ndeclare module 'hono' {\n  interface ContextVariableMap {\n    wsAuth: AuthContext;\n    wsClientIP: string;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction sendToClient(socket: WSContext, message: ServerMessage): void {\n  if (socket.readyState === 1) {\n    socket.send(JSON.stringify(message));\n  }\n}\n\nexport function durationSecondsForLinear16AudioBytes(byteLength: number): number {\n  return byteLength / (STREAMING_SAMPLE_RATE * STREAMING_CHANNELS * LINEAR16_BYTES_PER_SAMPLE);\n}\n\nfunction buildDeepgramUrl(language?: string, vocabulary?: string): string {\n  const params = new URLSearchParams({\n    model: 'nova-3',\n    smart_format: 'true',\n    interim_results: 'true',\n    punctuate: 'true',\n    endpointing: '300',\n    encoding: 'linear16',\n    sample_rate: '16000',\n    channels: '1',\n    mip_opt_out: 'true',\n  });\n\n  const normalizedLanguage = language?.toLowerCase();\n  if (normalizedLanguage && normalizedLanguage !== 'auto') {\n    params.set('language', normalizedLanguage);\n    if (vocabulary) {\n      // Nova-3 `keyterm` takes ONE repeated query value per term — `keyterm=a&\n      // keyterm=b` — and does NOT support a `:boost` suffix (that's the legacy\n      // nova-2 `keywords` syntax). A single comma-joined `a:1.5,b:1.5` value\n      // boosts one literal phrase and does nothing. Split/clean to match the\n      // REST adapter's convertToKeyterms, then append each term individually.\n      const terms = vocabulary\n        .split(/[,\\n;]+/)\n        .map(t => t.trim().replace(/^[-*]\\s*/, ''))\n        .filter(t => t.length > 0 && t.length <= 50)\n        .slice(0, 100);\n      for (const term of terms) {\n        params.append('keyterm', term);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return `wss://api.deepgram.com/v1/listen?${params.toString()}`;\n}\n\nexport async function wsStreamingPreflight(c: Context, next: Next) {\n  const upgradeHeader = c.req.header('Upgrade');\n  if (!upgradeHeader || upgradeHeader.toLowerCase() !== 'websocket') {\n    return c.text('Expected WebSocket upgrade', 426);\n  }\n\n  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n    return c.text('Access denied', 403);\n  }\n\n  const url = new URL(c.req.url);\n  // `account_key` is the canonical param; `license_key` is the legacy alias that\n  // installed native apps still send, so we accept either.\n  const licenseKey =\n    url.searchParams.get('account_key') ||\n    url.searchParams.get('license_key') ||\n    undefined;\n\n  if (!licenseKey) {\n    return c.text('Missing account_key', 401);\n  }\n\n  const authResult = await validateAuth({ licenseKey });\n  if (!authResult.ok) {\n    return c.text('Unauthorized', 401);\n  }\n\n  const creditCheck = await validateCredits(authResult.value, minimumStreamingCredits(), clientIP);\n  if (!creditCheck.ok) {\n    return creditCheck.response;\n  }\n\n  c.set('wsAuth', authResult.value);\n  c.set('wsClientIP', clientIP);\n\n  return next();\n}\n\nexport const wsStreamingRoute = upgradeWebSocket((c) => {\n  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n  const auth = c.get('wsAuth');\n  const clientIP = c.get('wsClientIP');\n  const url = new URL(c.req.url);\n  const language = url.searchParams.get('language') || undefined;\n  const vocabulary = url.searchParams.get('vocabulary') || undefined;\n  const apiKey = process.env.DEEPGRAM_API_KEY || '';\n\n  let totalDurationSeconds = 0;\n  let bytesReceived = 0;\n  let deepgramWs: WebSocket | null = null;\n  let sessionEnded = false;\n  let clientSocket: WSContext | null = null;\n  let pingInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;\n\n  const dgUrl = buildDeepgramUrl(language, vocabulary);\n\n  async function endSession(): Promise<void> {\n    if (sessionEnded) return;\n    sessionEnded = true;\n\n    if (pingInterval) {\n      clearInterval(pingInterval);\n      pingInterval = null;\n    }\n\n    const costUsd = computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(totalDurationSeconds);\n    const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n    if (clientSocket) {\n      sendToClient(clientSocket, {\n        type: 'session_complete',\n        duration_seconds: totalDurationSeconds,\n        credits_used: creditsUsed,\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (creditsUsed > 0) {\n      deductCredits(\n        auth,\n        costUsd,\n        {\n          audio_duration_seconds: totalDurationSeconds,\n          transcription_cost_usd: costUsd,\n          language: language || 'auto',\n          endpoint: '/ws/streaming-deepgram',\n          stt_provider: 'deepgram-nova3-live',\n        },\n        clientIP\n      ).catch(console.error);\n    }\n  }\n\n  function closeUpstream(): void {\n    // readyState 0 = CONNECTING, 1 = OPEN — close both so a client that\n    // disconnects mid-handshake doesn't leave the upstream socket open until\n    // Deepgram's idle timeout. close() during CONNECTING aborts the handshake\n    // once it completes; CLOSING/CLOSED need no action.\n    if (deepgramWs && deepgramWs.readyState <= WebSocket.OPEN) {\n      deepgramWs.close(1000, 'Client disconnected');\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    onOpen: (_evt, ws) => {\n      clientSocket = ws;\n\n      if (!apiKey) {\n        sendToClient(ws, { type: 'error', message: 'Deepgram API key not configured' });\n        ws.close(1011, 'Configuration error');\n        return;\n      }\n\n      deepgramWs = new WebSocket(dgUrl, ['token', apiKey]);\n\n      deepgramWs.addEventListener('open', () => {\n        // If the client already disconnected while we were still handshaking,\n        // tear down the upstream socket instead of leaving it orphaned.\n        if (ws.readyState !== 1) {\n          closeUpstream();\n          return;\n        }\n        sendToClient(ws, { type: 'ready', sessionId: requestId });\n      });\n\n      deepgramWs.addEventListener('message', (event) => {\n        try {\n          const data = JSON.parse(event.data as string) as DeepgramLiveResponse;\n          if (data.type === 'Results') {\n            const transcript = data.channel?.alternatives?.[0]?.transcript || '';\n            if (transcript || data.is_final) {\n              sendToClient(ws, {\n                type: 'transcript',\n                text: transcript,\n                is_final: data.is_final ?? false,\n                speech_final: data.speech_final ?? false,\n              });\n            }\n          }\n        } catch (error) {\n          console.warn('Failed to parse Deepgram message', error);\n        }\n      });\n\n      deepgramWs.addEventListener('error', () => {\n        sendToClient(ws, { type: 'error', message: 'Transcription service error' });\n      });\n\n      deepgramWs.addEventListener('close', async () => {\n        await endSession();\n        if (ws.readyState === 1) {\n          ws.close(1000, 'Session ended');\n        }\n      });\n\n      // Send ping every 30s to prevent Fly.io's 60s idle timeout from killing the connection\n      pingInterval = setInterval(() => {\n        if (clientSocket && clientSocket.readyState === 1) {\n          clientSocket.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'ping' }));\n        }\n      }, 30000);\n    },\n    onMessage: (event) => {\n      if (!deepgramWs || deepgramWs.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const data = event.data;\n      if (data instanceof ArrayBuffer) {\n        // Count every inbound frame toward the session total first — even ones\n        // we reject below — so a flood of oversized frames still trips the\n        // cumulative cap and closes the socket instead of looping forever.\n        bytesReceived += data.byteLength;\n\n        // Bound total inbound volume so a flood can't OOM the worker. Checked\n        // before the per-frame size guard so oversized frames also count here\n        // and a sustained flood reliably closes the connection.\n        if (bytesReceived > MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES) {\n          if (clientSocket) {\n            sendToClient(clientSocket, { type: 'error', message: 'Audio stream too large' });\n            clientSocket.close(1009, 'Message too big');\n          }\n          return;\n        }\n\n        // Reject an abnormally large single frame outright — never forward it.\n        if (data.byteLength > MAX_AUDIO_MESSAGE_BYTES) {\n          if (clientSocket) {\n            sendToClient(clientSocket, { type: 'error', message: 'Audio chunk too large' });\n          }\n          return;\n        }\n\n        // Backpressure: if the upstream socket is already congested, drop this\n        // chunk instead of queueing more memory into the outbound buffer.\n        if (deepgramWs.bufferedAmount > MAX_DEEPGRAM_BUFFERED_BYTES) {\n          if (clientSocket) {\n            sendToClient(clientSocket, { type: 'error', message: 'Transcription service busy, audio dropped' });\n          }\n          return;\n        }\n\n        deepgramWs.send(data);\n        totalDurationSeconds += durationSecondsForLinear16AudioBytes(data.byteLength);\n\n        // End the session once the running cost reaches the balance seen at auth,\n        // so a low-balance user can't stream indefinitely on end-of-session billing.\n        const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(totalDurationSeconds));\n        if (creditsUsed >= auth.credits) {\n          if (clientSocket) {\n            sendToClient(clientSocket, { type: 'error', message: 'Credit balance exhausted' });\n          }\n          deepgramWs.close(1000, 'Credits exhausted');\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (typeof data === 'string') {\n        try {\n          const msg = JSON.parse(data) as { type?: string };\n          if (msg.type === 'stop') {\n            deepgramWs.close(1000, 'Client requested stop');\n            return;\n          }\n          if (msg.type === 'pong') {\n            // Client pong response — ignore\n            return;\n          }\n        } catch {\n          // ignore non-JSON text messages\n        }\n      }\n    },\n    onClose: async () => {\n      await endSession();\n      closeUpstream();\n    },\n    onError: async () => {\n      if (clientSocket) {\n        sendToClient(clientSocket, { type: 'error', message: 'WebSocket error' });\n      }\n      await endSession();\n      closeUpstream();\n    },\n  };\n});\n","numLines":377,"startLine":1,"totalLines":377}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"087aae1e-4963-4b51-85f7-9827bb69e609","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

 };\n}\n\n/**\n * Calls the Anthropic Messages API with streaming enabled.\n * Returns a ReadableStream that emits OpenAI-compatible SSE chunks,\n * and a promise that resolves to the total cost in USD after the stream completes.\n */\nexport function streamAnthropicChat(\n  systemPrompt: string,\n  messages: AnthropicMessage[],\n  requestId: string\n): AnthropicStreamResult {\n  const apiKey = process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY;\n  if (!apiKey) {\n    throw new Error('ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not configured');\n  }\n\n  let resolveCost: (cost: number) => void;\n  const costPromise = new Promise<number>((resolve) => {\n    resolveCost = resolve;\n  });\n\n  const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n\n  // Abort the upstream Anthropic request when the client disconnects,\n  // so we stop paying for tokens nobody will receive.\n  const abortController = new AbortController();\n\n  // Hoisted so cancel() can bill the tokens consumed up to the abort point.\n  let inputTokens = 0;\n  let outputTokens = 0;\n\n  const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({\n    async start(controller) {\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(ANTHROPIC_API_URL, {\n          method: 'POST',\n          headers: {\n            'x-api-key': apiKey,\n            'anthropic-version': ANTHROPIC_VERSION,\n            'content-type': 'application/json',\n          },\n          body: JSON.stringify({\n            model: ANTHROPIC_MODEL,\n            max_tokens: ANTHROPIC_MAX_TOKENS,\n            system: systemPrompt,\n            messages,\n            stream: true,\n          }),\n          signal: abortController.signal,\n        });\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          const errorText = await response.text().catch(() => '');\n          console.error(`[${requestId}] Anthropic API error: ${response.status} ${errorText.slice(0, 500)}`);\n          const errorChunk = JSON.stringify({\n            choices: [{ delta: { content: '' }, finish_reason: 'error' }],\n            error: `Anthropic API error: ${response.status}`,\n          });\n          controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(`data: ${errorChunk}\\n\\n`));\n          controller.enqueue(encoder.encode('data: [DONE]\\n\\n'));\n          controller.close();\n          resolveCost(0);\n          return;\n        }\n\n        const body = response.body;\n        if (!body) {\n          controller.enqueue(encoder.encode('data: [DONE]\\n\\n'));\n          controller.close();\n          resolveCost(0);\n          return;\n        }\n\n        const reader = body.getReader();\n        const decoder = new TextDecoder();\n        let buffer = '';\n        // inputTokens/outputTokens are hoisted to function scope (so cancel()/catch\n        // can bill partial cost); the cache buckets are only needed in the success path.\n        let cacheCreationTokens = 0;\n        let cacheReadTokens = 0;\n\n        while (true) {\n          const { done, value } = await reader.read();\n          if (done) break;\n\n          buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });\n          const lines = buffer.split('\\n');\n          buffer = lines.pop() || '';\n\n          for (const line of lines) {\n            if (!line.startsWith('data: ')) continue;\n            const data = line.slice(6).trim();\n            if (!data || data === '[DONE]') continue;\n\n            try {\n              const event = JSON.parse(data);\n\n              // Track usage from message_start (cache_* buckets arrive here too)\n              if (event.type === 'message_start' && event.message?.usage) {\n                inputTokens = event.message.usage.input_tokens || 0;\n                cacheCreationTokens = event.message.usage.cache_creation_input_tokens || 0;\n                cacheReadTokens = event.message.usage.cache_read_input_tokens || 0;\n              }\n\n              // Emit text deltas as OpenAI-compatible chunks\n              if (event.type === 'content_block_delta' && event.delta?.text) {\n                const chunk = JSON.stringify({\n                  choices: [{ delta: { content: event.delta.text } }],\n                });\n                controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(`data: ${chunk}\\n\\n`));\n              }\n\n              // Track output tokens from message_delta\n              if (event.type === 'message_delta' && event.usage) {\n                outputTokens = event.usage.output_tokens || 0;\n              }\n            } catch {\n              // Skip malformed JSON lines\n            }\n          }\n        }\n\n        controller.enqueue(encoder.encode('data: [DONE]\\n\\n'));\n        controller.close();\n\n        const costUsd = computeAnthropicCost(inputTokens, outputTokens, cacheCreationTokens, cacheReadTokens);\n        console.log(`[${requestId}] Anthropic usage: input=${inputTokens}, output=${outputTokens}, cacheWrite=${cacheCreationTokens}, cacheRead=${cacheReadTokens}, cost=$${costUsd.toFixed(6)}`);\n        resolveCost(costUsd);\n      } catch (error) {\n        if (abortController.signal.aborted) {\n          // Client disconnected; cancel() already resolved the partial cost.\n          return;\n        }\n        // Bill the tokens observed up to the failure point. Anthropic still\n        // charges us for whatever it generated before the stream broke, so\n        // zeroing here under-bills (input tokens are known from message_start;\n        // output tokens reflect the last message_delta we saw, if any).\n        const costUsd = computeAnthropicCost(inputTokens, outputTokens);\n        console.error(`[${requestId}] Anthropic stream error: input=${inputTokens}, output=${outputTokens}, cost=$${costUsd.toFixed(6)}`, error);\n        try {\n          controller.enqueue(encoder.encode('data: [DONE]\\n\\n'));\n          controller.close();\n        } catch {\n          // Controller may already be closed\n        }\n        resolveCost(costUsd);\n      }\n    },\n    cancel(reason) {\n      // Client disconnected: abort the upstream Anthropic request and bill\n      // only the tokens observed up to this point (best effort — Anthropic\n      // reports output_tokens in the final message_delta, so a mid-stream\n      // disconnect typically bills input tokens only).\n      abortController.abort();\n      const costUsd = computeAnthropicCost(inputTokens, outputTokens);\n      console.log(`[${requestId}] Anthropic stream cancelled (client disconnect): reason=${String(reason ?? 'unknown')}, input=${inputTokens}, output=${outputTokens}, cost=$${costUsd.toFixed(6)}`);\n      resolveCost(costUsd);\n    },\n  });\n\n  return { stream, costPromise };\n}\n","numLines":165,"startLine":100,"totalLines":264}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"190c5c26-5abc-4fd3-b5ab-ec886461fb68","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"3b340824-b410-4346-a771-282d6cd13719","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c4d76306-3d6f-4795-97b1-6d7124e0032e","agentId":"a132c21869db6323d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UK1Fg24UQHPG5ndv2j1FVt","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// HYPERWHISPER FLY.IO TRANSCRIPTION SERVICE\n2\t// Edge-based transcription proxy replacing Cloudflare Workers\n3\t// Eliminates R2 upload path complexity - can buffer larger audio files in memory\n4\t\n5\timport { Hono } from 'hono';\n6\timport { cors } from 'hono/cors';\n7\timport { websocket } from 'hono/bun';\n8\timport { transcribeRoute } from './routes/transcribe';\n9\timport { postProcessRoute } from './routes/post-process';\n10\timport { assistantRoute } from './routes/assistant';\n11\timport { usageRoute } from './routes/usage';\n12\timport { wsStreamingPreflight, wsStreamingRoute } from './routes/ws-streaming-deepgram';\n13\timport { drainPendingDeductions } from './middleware/credits';\n14\t\n15\tconst app = new Hono();\n16\t\n17\t// CORS — allow the custom STT selection headers so browser callers can set\n18\t// provider/model/domain. Without these in allowHeaders the preflight blocks the\n19\t// POST before it reaches the route (X-STT-* are non-simple request headers).\n20\tapp.use('*', cors({\n21\t  origin: '*',\n22\t  allowHeaders: ['Content-Type', 'X-STT-Provider', 'X-STT-Model', 'X-STT-Domain'],\n23\t  allowMethods: ['GET', 'POST', 'OPTIONS'],\n24\t}));\n25\t\n26\tapp.options('*', (c) => c.body(null, 204));\n27\t\n28\t// Health check endpoint - Fly.io uses this for health monitoring\n29\tapp.get('/health', (c) => {\n30\t  return c.json({\n31\t    status: 'ok',\n32\t    region: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n33\t    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n34\t  });\n35\t});\n36\t\n37\t// Warmup endpoint - clients hit this on hotkey-down to pre-establish\n38\t// the TLS/HTTP2 connection before the /transcribe POST.\n39\tapp.get('/warmup', (c) => {\n40\t  c.header('Cache-Control', 'no-store');\n41\t  return c.body(null, 204);\n42\t});\n43\t\n44\t// Main transcription endpoint\n45\tapp.post('/transcribe', transcribeRoute);\n46\t\n47\t// Standalone post-processing endpoint\n48\tapp.post('/post-process', postProcessRoute);\n49\t\n50\t// Assistant mode endpoint (vision LLM)\n51\tapp.post('/assistant', assistantRoute);\n52\t\n53\t// Usage endpoint\n54\tapp.get('/usage', usageRoute);\n55\t\n56\t// WebSocket streaming endpoint\n57\tapp.get('/ws/streaming-deepgram', wsStreamingPreflight, wsStreamingRoute);\n58\t\n59\t// Fallback - match Cloudflare (405, plain text)\n60\tapp.notFound((c) => {\n61\t  return c.text('Method not allowed', 405);\n62\t});\n63\t\n64\t// Error handler — never echo raw err.message to the client (it can contain\n65\t// env-var names, upstream provider bodies, request IDs). Log the full error\n66\t// server-side with an error_id the client can quote to support.\n67\tapp.onError((err, c) => {\n68\t  const errorId = crypto.randomUUID();\n69\t  console.error(`Unhandled error [${errorId}]:`, err);\n70\t  return c.json({\n71\t    error: 'Internal server error',\n72\t    message: 'An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.',\n73\t    error_id: errorId,\n74\t  }, 500);\n75\t});\n76\t\n77\t// Graceful shutdown: Fly sends SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down with a grace\n78\t// period before SIGKILL (kill_timeout, default 5s). Credit deductions are\n79\t// fired without awaiting after the response is flushed, so drain any that are\n80\t// still in flight before exiting — otherwise the user keeps the transcript\n81\t// but the charge is silently dropped.\n82\tconst SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS = 4_000;\n83\tlet shuttingDown = false;\n84\t\n85\tasync function gracefulShutdown(signal: string): Promise<void> {\n86\t  if (shuttingDown) return;\n87\t  shuttingDown = true;\n88\t\n89\t  console.log('machine.shutdown', {\n90\t    signal,\n91\t    machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n92\t    shutdownAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n93\t  });\n94\t\n95\t  const drained = await drainPendingDeductions(SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS);\n96\t  if (drained > 0) {\n97\t    console.log('machine.shutdown_drained_deductions', { count: drained });\n98\t  }\n99\t  process.exit(0);\n100\t}\n101\t\n102\tprocess.on('SIGTERM', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGTERM'); Without these in allowHeaders the preflight blocks the\n// POST before it reaches the route (X-STT-* are non-simple request headers).\napp.use('*', cors({\n  origin: '*',\n  allowHeaders: ['Content-Type', 'X-STT-Provider', 'X-STT-Model', 'X-STT-Domain'],\n  allowMethods: ['GET', 'POST', 'OPTIONS'],\n}));\n\napp.options('*', (c) => c.body(null, 204));\n\n// Health check endpoint - Fly.io uses this for health monitoring\napp.get('/health', (c) => {\n  return c.json({\n    status: 'ok',\n    region: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n});\n\n// Warmup endpoint - clients hit this on hotkey-down to pre-establish\n// the TLS/HTTP2 connection before the /transcribe POST.\napp.get('/warmup', (c) => {\n  c.header('Cache-Control', 'no-store');\n  return c.body(null, 204);\n});\n\n// Main transcription endpoint\napp.post('/transcribe', transcribeRoute);\n\n// Standalone post-processing endpoint\napp.post('/post-process', postProcessRoute);\n\n// Assistant mode endpoint (vision LLM)\napp.post('/assistant', assistantRoute);\n\n// Usage endpoint\napp.get('/usage', usageRoute);\n\n// WebSocket streaming endpoint\napp.get('/ws/streaming-deepgram', wsStreamingPreflight, wsStreamingRoute);\n\n// Fallback - match Cloudflare (405, plain text)\napp.notFound((c) => {\n  return c.text('Method not allowed', 405);\n});\n\n// Error handler — never echo raw err.message to the client (it can contain\n// env-var names, upstream provider bodies, request IDs). Log the full error\n// server-side with an error_id the client can quote to support.\napp.onError((err, c) => {\n  const errorId = crypto.randomUUID();\n  console.error(`Unhandled error [${errorId}]:`, err);\n  return c.json({\n    error: 'Internal server error',\n    message: 'An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.',\n    error_id: errorId,\n  }, 500);\n});\n\n// Graceful shutdown: Fly sends SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down with a grace\n// period before SIGKILL (kill_timeout, default 5s). Credit deductions are\n// fired without awaiting after the response is flushed, so drain any that are\n// still in flight before exiting — otherwise the user keeps the transcript\n// but the charge is silently dropped.\nconst SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS = 4_000;\nlet shuttingDown = false;\n\nasync function gracefulShutdown(signal: string): Promise<void> {\n  if (shuttingDown) return;\n  shuttingDown = true;\n\n  console.log('machine.shutdown', {\n    signal,\n    machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n    shutdownAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n\n  const drained = await drainPendingDeductions(SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS);\n  if (drained > 0) {\n    console.log('machine.shutdown_drained_deductions', { count: drained });\n  }\n  process.exit(0);\n}\n\nprocess.on('SIGTERM', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGTERM'); });\nprocess.on('SIGINT', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGINT'); });\n\n// Export for Bun\nexport default {\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080,\n  fetch: app.fetch,\n  websocket,\n};\n\nconsole.log('machine.boot', {\n  region: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n  machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n  allocId: process.env.FLY_ALLOC_ID || undefined,\n  imageRef: process.env.FLY_IMAGE_REF || undefined,\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080,\n  bootAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n});\nconsole.log(`HyperWhisper Fly.io service starting on port ${process.env.PORT || 8080}`);\nconsole.log(`Region: ${process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local'}`);\nconsole.log(`License API: ${process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || 'https://www.hyperwhisper.com (default)'}`);\nconsole.log('Started server: http://localhost:' + (process.env.PORT || 8080));\n","numLines":124,"startLine":1,"totalLines":124}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3b340824-b410-4346-a771-282d6cd13719","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

};\n17\t\n18\texport interface CreditEstimateOptions {\n19\t  costEstimators?: Array<(durationSeconds: number) => number>;\n20\t}\n21\t\n22\texport function estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes: estimateCreditsFromSize(sizeBytes: number, options: CreditEstimateOptions = {}): number {\n28\t  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n29\t\n30\t  if (options.costEstimators?.length) {\n31\t    const maxEstimatedCost = Math.max(\n32\t      ...options.costEstimators.map((estimateCost) => estimateCost(estimatedSeconds))\n33\t    );\n34\t    return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(maxEstimatedCost));\n35\t  }\n36\t\n37\t  const estimatedCredits = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * CREDITS_PER_MINUTE;\n38\t  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(estimatedCredits));\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\texport async function validateCredits(\n42\t  auth: AuthContext,\n43\t  estimatedCredits: number,\n44\t  _clientIP: string\n45\t): Promise<CreditsResult> {\n46\t  const balance = roundToTenth(auth.credits);\n47\t  if (balance < estimatedCredits) {\n48\t    return { ok: false, response: insufficientCreditsResponse(balance, estimatedCredits) };\n49\t  }\n50\t  return { ok: true };\n51\t}\n52\t\n53\tasync function recordLicenseUsage(\n54\t  licenseKey: string,\n55\t  creditsUsed: number,\n56\t  metadata: Record<string, unknown>\n57\t): Promise<void> {\n58\t  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n59\t\n60\t  try {\n61\t    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/license/credits`, {\n62\t      method: 'POST',\n63\t      headers: {\n64\t        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n65\t      },\n66\t      body: JSON.stringify({\n67\t        license_key: licenseKey,\n68\t        amount: creditsUsed,\n69\t        metadata,\n70\t      }),\n71\t      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n72\t    });\n73\t\n74\t    if (!response.ok) {\n75\t      const errorData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));\n76\t      console.warn('POST /api/license/credits failed', {\n77\t        status: response.status,\n78\t        error: (errorData as Record<string, unknown>).error || 'Unknown error',\n79\t        creditsUsed,\n80\t      });\n81\t      return;\n82\t    }\n83\t\n84\t    const data = await response.json() as { credits_remaining?: number; credits_deducted?: number };\n85\t    if (typeof data.credits_remaining === 'number') {\n86\t      await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n87\t        isValid: true,\n88\t        credits: data.credits_remaining,\n89\t        cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n90\t      });\n91\t    }\n92\t  } catch (error) {\n93\t    console.warn('POST /api/license/credits network error', {\n94\t      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n95\t    });\n96\t  }\n97\t}\n98\t\n99\t// In-flight deduction tracking for graceful shutdown.\n100\t// Call sites fire deductCredits() without awaiting (response latency), so a\n101\t// Fly machine recycle (SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down) between the response\n102\t// flush and the redis/license write would silently drop the charge. Every\n103\t// deduction registers here so the SIGTERM handler can drain before exit.\n104\tconst inFlightDeductions = new Set<Promise<number>>();\n105\t\n106\texport async function drainPendingDeductions(timeoutMs: number): Promise<number> {\n107\t  const pendingCount = inFlightDeductions.size;\n108\t  if (pendingCount === 0) {\n109\t    return 0;\n110\t  }\n111\t\n112\t  const allSettled = Promise.allSettled([...inFlightDeductions]);\n113\t  const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));\n114\t  await Promise.race([allSettled, timeout]);\n115\t  return pendingCount;\n116\t}\n117\t\n118\texport function deductCredits(\n119\t  auth: AuthContext,\n120\t  costUsd: number,\n121\t  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n122\t  clientIP: string\n123\t): Promise<number> {\n124\t  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n125\t  inFlightDeductions.add(deduction);\n126\t  deduction\n127\t    .catch(() => {}) // errors are logged inside performDeduction / by callers\n128\t    .finally(() => inFlightDeductions.delete(deduction));\n129\t  return deduction;\n130\t}\n131\t\n132\tasync function performDeduction(\n133\t  auth: AuthContext,\n134\t  costUsd: number,\n135\t  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n136\t  _clientIP: string\n137\t): Promise<number> {\n138\t  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n139\t\n140\t  if (creditsUsed <= 0) {\n141\t    return 0;\n142\t  }\n143\t\n144\t  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\n145\t  return Array<(durationSeconds: number) => number>;\n}\n\nexport function estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes: number): number {\n  const estimatedMinutes = sizeBytes / BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE;\n  return Math.max(MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS, estimatedMinutes * 60);\n}\n\nexport function estimateCreditsFromSize(sizeBytes: number, options: CreditEstimateOptions = {}): number {\n  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n\n  if (options.costEstimators?.length) {\n    const maxEstimatedCost = Math.max(\n      ...options.costEstimators.map((estimateCost) => estimateCost(estimatedSeconds))\n    );\n    return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(maxEstimatedCost));\n  }\n\n  const estimatedCredits = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * CREDITS_PER_MINUTE;\n  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(estimatedCredits));\n}\n\nexport async function validateCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  estimatedCredits: number,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<CreditsResult> {\n  const balance = roundToTenth(auth.credits);\n  if (balance < estimatedCredits) {\n    return { ok: false, response: insufficientCreditsResponse(balance, estimatedCredits) };\n  }\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nasync function recordLicenseUsage(\n  licenseKey: string,\n  creditsUsed: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>\n): Promise<void> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/license/credits`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        amount: creditsUsed,\n        metadata,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      const errorData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      console.warn('POST /api/license/credits failed', {\n        status: response.status,\n        error: (errorData as Record<string, unknown>).error || 'Unknown error',\n        creditsUsed,\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const data = await response.json() as { credits_remaining?: number; credits_deducted?: number };\n    if (typeof data.credits_remaining === 'number') {\n      await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n        isValid: true,\n        credits: data.credits_remaining,\n        cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      });\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn('POST /api/license/credits network error', {\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// In-flight deduction tracking for graceful shutdown.\n// Call sites fire deductCredits() without awaiting (response latency), so a\n// Fly machine recycle (SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down) between the response\n// flush and the redis/license write would silently drop the charge. Every\n// deduction registers here so the SIGTERM handler can drain before exit.\nconst inFlightDeductions = new Set<Promise<number>>();\n\nexport async function drainPendingDeductions(timeoutMs: number): Promise<number> {\n  const pendingCount = inFlightDeductions.size;\n  if (pendingCount === 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  const allSettled = Promise.allSettled([...inFlightDeductions]);\n  const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));\n  await Promise.race([allSettled, timeout]);\n  return pendingCount;\n}\n\nexport function deductCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n  inFlightDeductions.add(deduction);\n  deduction\n    .catch(() => {}) // errors are logged inside performDeduction / by callers\n    .finally(() => inFlightDeductions.delete(deduction));\n  return deduction;\n}\n\nasync function performDeduction(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n  if (creditsUsed <= 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\n  return creditsUsed;\n}\n","numLines":147,"startLine":1,"totalLines":147}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5eb5a23f-47f2-4f80-9177-f11a31123979","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

'image/webp',\n]);\n\nfunction normalizeImageMediaType(mediaType: string | null | undefined): string | null {\n  const normalized = mediaType?.split(';', 1)[0]?.trim().toLowerCase();\n  return normalized && SUPPORTED_IMAGE_MEDIA_TYPES.has(normalized) ? normalized : null;\n}\n\nexport function detectImageMediaType(bytes: Uint8Array): string | null {\n  if (bytes.length >= 3 && bytes[0] === 0xff && bytes[1] === 0xd8 && bytes[2] === 0xff) {\n    return 'image/jpeg';\n  }\n\n  if (\n    bytes.length >= 8\n    && bytes[0] === 0x89\n    && bytes[1] === 0x50\n    && bytes[2] === 0x4e\n    && bytes[3] === 0x47\n    && bytes[4] === 0x0d\n    && bytes[5] === 0x0a\n    && bytes[6] === 0x1a\n    && bytes[7] === 0x0a\n  ) {\n    return 'image/png';\n  }\n\n  if (\n    bytes.length >= 6\n    && 'image/gif';\n  }\n\n  if (\n    bytes.length >= 12\n    && bytes[0] === 0x52\n    && bytes[1] === 0x49\n    && bytes[2] === 0x46\n    && bytes[3] === 0x46\n    && bytes[8] === 0x57\n    && bytes[9] === 0x45\n    && bytes[10] === 0x42\n    && bytes[11] === 0x50\n  ) {\n    return 'image/webp';\n  }\n\n  return null;\n}\n\nexport function validateAssistantContentLength(contentLengthHeader: string | undefined):\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response } {\n  if (!contentLengthHeader) {\n    return { ok: false, response: errorResponse(400, 'Missing Content-Length', 'Content-Length header is required') };\n  }\n\n  const contentLength = Number.parseInt(contentLengthHeader, 10);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(contentLength) || contentLength <= 0) {\n    return { ok: false, response: Each 4 base64 chars decode to 3 bytes; trailing `=` padding reduces\n * the final group. Good enough to enforce a size cap before forwarding.\n */\nfunction estimateBase64DecodedBytes(base64: string): number {\n  const len = base64.length;\n  if (len === 0) return 0;\n  let padding = 0;\n  if (base64.endsWith('==')) padding = 2;\n  else if (base64.endsWith('=')) padding = 1;\n  return Math.floor((len * 3) / 4) - padding;\n}\n\n/**\n * Convert the client's OpenAI-format messages to Anthropic Messages API format.\n * - Extracts the system message (returned separately for Anthropic's `system` param)\n * - Converts image_url content blocks to Anthropic image blocks\n */\nexport function convertMessages(\n  clientMessages: unknown[],\n  imageBase64: string | null,\n  imageMediaType = DEFAULT_IMAGE_MEDIA_TYPE\n): { systemPrompt: string; messages: AnthropicMessage[]; imageCount: number } {\n  let systemPrompt = '';\n  const messages: AnthropicMessage[] = [];\n  // Cap total images (multipart + inline) forwarded to Anthropic so a client\n  // cannot drive unbounded vision spend by embedding many image blocks.\n  let imageCount = 0;\n\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    if (!msg || typeof msg !== 'object') continue;\n    const m = msg as Record<string, unknown>;\n    const role = m.role as string;\n\n    if (role === 'system') {\n      systemPrompt = typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : '';\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    if (role !== 'user' && role !== 'assistant') continue;\n\n    // Handle string content\n    if (typeof m.content === 'string') {\n      messages.push({ role, content: m.content });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Handle array content (multimodal — image_url + text)\n    if (Array.isArray(m.content)) {\n      const blocks: AnthropicContentBlock[] = [];\n\n      for (const part of m.content) {\n        if (!part || typeof part !== 'object') continue;\n        const p = part as Record<string, unknown>;\n\n        if (p.type === 'text' && typeof p.text === 'string') {\n          blocks.push({ type: 'text', text: p.text });\n        }\n\n        if (p.type === 'image_url') {\n          // Drop any images beyond the per-request cap instead of forwarding\n          // them to Anthropic (unbounded vision cost).\n          if (imageCount >= ASSISTANT_MAX_IMAGES) continue;\n\n          // The client embeds base64 inline as data:image/jpeg;base64,...\n          // But for HyperWhisper Cloud, the image comes as a separate multipart file\n          // We use the multipart image if available, otherwise parse inline\n          let base64Data = imageBase64;\n          let mediaType = normalizeImageMediaType(imageMediaType) || DEFAULT_IMAGE_MEDIA_TYPE;\n\n          if (!base64Data && p.image_url && typeof p.image_url === 'object') {\n            const urlObj = p.image_url as Record<string, unknown>;\n            const url = urlObj.url as string;\n            if (url?.startsWith('data:')) {\n              const match = url.match(/^data:(image\\/\\w+);base64,(.+)$/);\n              if (match) {\n                const parsedMediaType = normalizeImageMediaType(match[1]);\n                // Reject oversized inline images. setSelectedPlatform(\"windows\");\n\n      return;\n    }\n    if (platformParam === \"mac\") {\n      setSelectedPlatform(\"mac\");\n\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Fall back to OS detection\n    const platform = navigator.platform?.toLowerCase() || \"\";\n    const userAgent = navigator.userAgent?.toLowerCase() || \"\";\n\n    if (platform.includes(\"win\") || userAgent.includes(\"windows\")) {\n      setSelectedPlatform(\"windows\");\n    }\n    // macOS is default, no change needed\n  }, [searchParams]);\n\n  // Fetch the download URL on mount (but don't trigger download yet)\n  // Skip  redirect: \"follow\",\n          },\n        );\n        const url =\n          response.url || `/api/download?platform=${selectedPlatform}`;\n\n        setDownloadState((prev) => ({\n          ...prev,\n          [selectedPlatform]: { ...prev[selectedPlatform], url },\n        }));\n      } catch (error) {\n        console.error(\"Failed to fetch download URL:\", error);\n        setDownloadState((prev) => ({\n          ...prev,\n          [selectedPlatform]: {\n            ...prev[selectedPlatform],\n            url: `/api/download?platform=${selectedPlatform}`,\n {"parentUuid":"05b7fba4-fd4e-4e86-b373-423ae8708ab5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a132c21869db6323d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PkAD1eTdESdEFhonrdfXu4","type":"tool_result","content":"  // Defaults to visible so SSR and first client render match; hidden after\n  // mount if a prior dismissal is stored.\n  const [bannerDismissed, setBannerDismissed] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (localStorage.getItem(\"os-banner-dismissed\") === \"1\") {\n      setBannerDismissed(true);\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const dismissBanner = () => {\n    setBannerDismissed(true);\n    localStorage.setItem(\"os-banner-dismissed\", \"1\");\n  };\n\n  // Check if this is a full-screen route (user portal)\n  const isFullScreenRoute = pathname.includes(\"/user\");\n\n  if (isFullScreenRoute) {\n    // Full-screen layout: no navbar/footer\n    return <div className=\"min-h-screen\">{children}</div>;\n  }\n\n  const showBanner = !bannerDismissed;\n\n  // Regular layout: with navbar and footer\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative flex flex-col min-h-screen\">\n      {/*\n        Sticky header holds the announcement banner + navbar. {"parentUuid":"c0cbfdf5-1b91-4d7b-80c0-912cc7d8db93","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a132c21869db6323d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01P4bZUxtjGFYscF8sm6kG7n","type":"tool_result","content":"DownloadModalContext.tsx\nPostHogProvider.tsx\n=== AUTH MW ===\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        include_credits: true,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    const responseText = await response.text();\n    let data: { valid?: boolean; credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n\n    try {\n      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n    } catch {\n      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n    }\n\n    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n\n    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n\n    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n    // retries the API. Throws if the bucket isn't configured — callers should\n98\t * gate with `isGcsTranscriptionBucketConfigured()` first.\n99\t */\n100\texport async function uploadTranscriptionAudio(\n101\t  audio: ArrayBuffer,\n102\t  contentType: string,\n103\t): Promise<TranscriptionAudioUpload> {\n104\t  const bucket = getConfiguredBucket();\n105\t  if (!bucket) {\n106\t    throw new Error('GOOGLE_SPEECH_GCS_BUCKET not configured');\n107\t  }\n108\t\n109\t  const ext = inferExtension(contentType);\n110\t  const objectName = buildObjectName(ext);\n111\t  const accessToken = await getGoogleAccessToken();\n112\t\n113\t  // Simple media upload (single request, no resumable session). {"parentUuid":"777d7392-63dd-4ec3-81a1-63b8e8626ef2","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a132c21869db6323d","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWJ6VrVVsBhdQ9aCbZwt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've read the WS bridge end to end, all `setInterval`/`getReader`/`AbortController` sites in both services, every `!response.ok` handler in the cloud providers, and all 14 React `useEffect` sites. Findings:\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/anthropic.ts`\nLine: 230\nInvariant: I11\nClaim: `streamAnthropicChat`'s `start()` catch block bills partial cost and closes the outbound controller but never calls `reader.cancel()` or `abortController.abort()` on the non-client-abort branch, so the upstream Anthropic response body acquired at line 175 stays locked and the fetch stays live.\nFailure scenario: A `/assistant` SSE response whose *outbound* stream breaks mid-flight (Fly proxy drops the connection, or `controller.enqueue` at line 211 throws `TypeError: The stream controller cannot close or enqueue` after the response was errored) lands in this catch with `abortController.signal.aborted === false`. Anthropic keeps generating up to `ANTHROPIC_MAX_TOKENS` into a body no one reads and no one aborts, holding the socket and its buffers for the full generation. Under repeated assistant failures on the 1 GB Fly machine this accumulates until the worker is OOM-killed, taking every concurrent transcription with it — and the user is billed for tokens that were never delivered.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/google-chirp.ts`\nLine: 616\nInvariant: I11\nClaim: `pollOperation` returns `{ done: false }` on a 429/5xx poll response without consuming or cancelling `response.body`, and this runs inside the batch-recognize poll loop.\nFailure scenario: A Google Speech batch job that is being throttled (429) polls every tick for the life of the job; each tick leaks one un-drained HTTP response body. A long audio file transcribed via `google-chirp` during a Google-side throttling window accumulates dozens of held connections on one request, exhausting the Fly machine's file-descriptor budget and making concurrent `/transcribe` calls fail with network errors that cascade through `FALLBACK_CHAINS`.\nConfidence: medium\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/gcs-storage.ts`\nLine: 163\nInvariant: I11\nClaim: The success path of `uploadTranscriptionAudio` logs and returns without ever reading `response.body` — the GCS object-metadata JSON is never drained. (Only the `!response.ok` branch at 146 reads it.)\nFailure scenario: Every successful Google Chirp transcription leaks one un-consumed GCS response body. The connection can't return to the keep-alive pool until GC runs, so a burst of Chirp traffic on the 1 GB machine drives socket/FD growth that is invisible until subsequent GCS uploads start failing with network errors and the whole chirp chain 502s.\nConfidence: medium\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/gcs-storage.ts`\nLine: 209\nInvariant: I11\nClaim: `deleteTranscriptionAudio` reads the body only on the `!response.ok && status !== 404` branch; the success and 404 paths return with the body un-consumed.\nFailure scenario: Same class as above — one leaked body per Chirp cleanup, doubling the per-request leak rate of the upload path. Concretely: sustained Chirp usage grows resident connections until the machine hits its FD limit and unrelated provider calls start throwing `network error`.\nConfidence: low\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/google-chirp.ts`\nLine: 658\nInvariant: I11\nClaim: `cancelBatchOperation` awaits `fetchWithTimeout`, logs `response.status`, and never consumes the body — and it runs from the outer `finally` on *every* Chirp failure path.\nFailure scenario: The cleanup path that runs on every aborted/failed Chirp transcription itself leaks a body. A user retrying a failing large upload repeatedly produces one leaked connection per attempt, on the exact path that is supposed to be releasing resources.\nConfidence: low\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts`\nLine: 308\nInvariant: I11\nClaim: The `MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES` breach closes the client side (`clientSocket.close(1009)`) but does not call `closeUpstream()`/`deepgramWs.close()` — teardown of the upstream Deepgram socket depends entirely on Bun delivering `onClose` for a server-initiated close.\nFailure scenario: A client that floods >100 MB and then refuses to complete the WebSocket closing handshake keeps the connection in CLOSING while the upstream Deepgram socket stays OPEN. Deepgram only tears it down after its own ~10 s no-audio idle timeout, and until then the socket, its buffers, and the still-running `pingInterval` are held. Repeated by a malicious client this is a cheap way to pin Fly file descriptors. Note: I could not fully rule out the transitive `onClose` path (see Coverage) — if Bun does fire `close`, this self-heals and the only cost is the extra idle-timeout window.\nConfidence: low\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/credits.ts`\nLine: 113\nInvariant: I11\nClaim: `drainPendingDeductions` races `Promise.allSettled` against `new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs))` and never clears the timer when `allSettled` wins.\nFailure scenario: The 4 s timer survives the race and keeps the event loop referenced. In the production SIGTERM path `process.exit(0)` immediately follows so there is no user-visible effect; the concrete consequence is in any non-exiting caller (tests, or a future in-process drain) where the process hangs for 4 s after all deductions have already settled.\nConfidence: low\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `nextjs/middleware.ts`\nLine: 58\nInvariant: I11\nClaim: `if (!response.ok) return null;` discards the `/api/auth/get-session` response without consuming its body; the same pattern appears at `nextjs/lib/services/model-list.ts:28` (6-provider fan-out) and `nextjs/app/api/download/route.ts:55`.\nFailure scenario: On an auth-service blip, every request to `/{locale}/user/customers` leaves an un-drained body in the middleware runtime. `model-list.ts:28` is worse: a cache-miss refresh with several failing providers leaks up to 6 bodies per invocation, keeping the Vercel function's sockets open for the remainder of its `maxDuration` and slowing the cold-start fan-out that is already unbounded.\nConfidence: low\n\n### Coverage\n\n**Examined buckets:** B1 (cloud-entry-and-auth — `index.ts` SIGTERM, `middleware/auth.ts`, `middleware/credits.ts`, `lib/redis.ts`, `lib/responses.ts`), B2 (cloud-routes — all five routes read for early returns, buffered bodies, and stream ownership), B3 (cloud-stt-providers — every `!response.ok` handler plus `providers/utils.ts` `fetchWithTimeout`), B4 (cloud-llm-providers — all seven LLM adapters' error paths plus `lib/llm-completion.ts`), B5 (cloud-billing-and-google — `gcs-storage.ts`, `google-auth.ts`, `google-chirp.ts` full poll/cancel/finally structure), B7/B9 (the fetch and DB-pool surfaces: `middleware.ts`, `lib/services/model-list.ts`, `app/api/download/route.ts`, `src/db/index.ts`, `src/db/migrate.ts`), B10/B11 (all 14 React `useEffect` sites in `app/`, `components/`, `contexts/`).\n\n**Not examined / partially examined:** B6 (next-money-api), B8 (next-trpc-server), B12 (next-tests) — swept by grep for every I11 idiom (`setInterval`/`setTimeout`/`getReader`/`releaseLock`/`.cancel(`/`AbortController`/`addEventListener`) with zero hits, so no enclosing functions were read. `nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts` was checked only for manual `pool.connect()`/`release()` (none — all access goes through drizzle's managed `db.transaction`), not read line by line.\n\n**Leads refuted:**\n- **`pingInterval` set without a matching `endSession()`** — refuted. The interval is set at the *end* of `onOpen` (line 287), after the only early return in that handler (the `!apiKey` branch at 238, which returns before `deepgramWs` is even constructed). Every subsequent exit — `onClose`, `onError`, and the Deepgram `close` listener — calls `endSession()`, which clears it under the `sessionEnded` guard.\n- **The `!apiKey` early return at line 238 orphaning resources** — refuted.
